   2338: function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
   2339:   /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
   2340:   const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   2341:   if (!match) return dateKey(today);
   2342:   const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
   2343:   const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
   2344:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
   2345:   return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
   2346: }
   2347: function attendanceDateControls(label,actionHtml="") {
   2348:   return `<div class="attendance-date-controls"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="-1">‹</button><div class="attendance-date-input-wrap"><input type="date" id="attendanceViewDate" value="${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate, true))}</strong></div><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="1">›</button><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-today>오늘</button>${actionHtml?`<div class="attendance-date-action">${actionHtml}</div>`:""}</div>`;
   2349: }
   2350: function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
   2351:   const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
   2352:   // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
   2353:   if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
   2354:   if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
   2355:   if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
   2356:   return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
   2357: }
   2358: function minutesOfClock(value) {
   2359:   const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
   2360:   return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
   2361: }
   2362: function slotRange(slot) {
   2363:   return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
   2364: }
   2365: function isWednesdayKey(value) {
   2366:   const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
   2367:   if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
   2368:     const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
   2369:     return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
   2370:   }
   2371:   return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
   2372: }
   2373: function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
   2374:   const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
   2375:   if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
   2376:   const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
   2377:   if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
   2378:   // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
   2379:   const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
   2380:   if(raw===true)return true;
   2381:   if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
   2382:   return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
   2383: }
   2384: function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
   2385:   const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
   2386:   for(const text of candidates){
   2387:     const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
   2388:     if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
   2389:   }
   2390:   return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
   2391: }
   2392: function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
   2393:   if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
   2394:   const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
   2395:   // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
   2396:   // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
   2397:   if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
   2398:   const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
   2399:     const text=String(value||"");
   2400:     return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
   2401:   });
   2402:   if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);

    190: function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
    191:   if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
    192:   if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
    193:   if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);

    199:       out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    200:     }
    201:     return out;
    202:   }
    203:   return String(value);
    204: }

   2352:   // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
   2353:   if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
   2354:   if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
   2355:   if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
   2356:   return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
   2357: }
   2358: function minutesOfClock(value) {
   2359:   const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
   2360:   return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
   2361: }
   2362: function slotRange(slot) {
   2363:   return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
   2364: }
   2365: function isWednesdayKey(value) {
   2366:   const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
   2367:   if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
   2368:     const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
   2369:     return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
   2370:   }
   2371:   return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
   2372: }
   2373: function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
   2374:   const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
   2375:   if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
   2376:   const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
   2377:   if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
   2378:   // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
   2379:   const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
   2380:   if(raw===true)return true;
   2381:   if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
   2382:   return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
   2383: }
   2384: function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
   2385:   const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
   2386:   for(const text of candidates){
   2387:     const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
   2388:     if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
   2389:   }
   2390:   return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
   2391: }
   2392: function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
   2393:   if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
   2394:   const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
   2395:   // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
   2396:   // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
   2397:   if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
   2398:   const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
   2399:     const text=String(value||"");
   2400:     return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
   2401:   });
   2402:   if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
   2403:   const range = slotRange(slot);
   2404:   const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
   2405:   if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
   2406:   const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
   2407:   if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
   2408:   const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
   2409:   return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
   2410: }

   2411: function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
   2412:   const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
   2413:   const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
   2414:   const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after");
   2415:   const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
   2416:   const seen = new Set();
   2417:   const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
   2418:     const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
   2419:     if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
   2420:   });
   2421:   for (const program of programs) {
   2422:     if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
   2423:     if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
   2424:     const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
   2425:     if (!target) continue;
   2426:     const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
   2427:     const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
   2428:     // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
   2429:     // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
   2430:     const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
   2431:     return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
   2432:   }
   2433:   return null;
   2434: }

   2440:   return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
   2441: }

   7520: function schoolPeriodStatus(now) {
   7521:   const minute = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
   7522:   const periods = [[520,570],[580,630],[640,690],[700,750],[820,870],[880,930],[940,990]];
   7523:   const index = periods.findIndex(([start, end]) => minute >= start && minute <= end);
   7524:   if (index >= 0) return { index, label: `현재 ${index + 1}교시` };
   7525:   if (minute < periods[0][0]) return { index: -1, label: "일과 시작 전" };
   7526:   if (minute > periods[6][1]) return { index: -1, label: "정규수업 종료" };

   2350: function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
   2351:   const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
   2352:   // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
   2353:   if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
   2354:   if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
   2355:   if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
   2356:   return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
   2357: }
   2358: function minutesOfClock(value) {
   2359:   const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
   2360:   return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
   2361: }
   2362: function slotRange(slot) {
   2363:   return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
   2364: }
   2365: function isWednesdayKey(value) {
   2366:   const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
   2367:   if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
   2368:     const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
   2369:     return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
   2370:   }
   2371:   return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
   2372: }
   2373: function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {

   2380:   if(raw===true)return true;
   2381:   if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
   2382:   return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());

   2411: function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
   2412:   const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
   2413:   const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");

   2416:   const seen = new Set();

   2418:     const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
   2419:     if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
   2420:   });

   2431:     return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
   2432:   }
   2433:   return null;

   2828: function inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue='',fileName=''){

   2836:   return {student:null,matchStatus:'확인필요'};

   7520: function schoolPeriodStatus(now) {
   7521:   const minute = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
   7522:   const periods = [[520,570],[580,630],[640,690],[700,750],[820,870],[880,930],[940,990]];
   7523:   const index = periods.findIndex(([start, end]) => minute >= start && minute <= end);
   7524:   if (index >= 0) return { index, label: `현재 ${index + 1}교시` };
   7525:   if (minute < periods[0][0]) return { index: -1, label: "일과 시작 전" };
   7526:   if (minute > periods[6][1]) return { index: -1, label: "정규수업 종료" };

   2338: function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
   2339:   /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
   2340:   const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   2341:   if (!match) return dateKey(today);
   2342:   const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
   2343:   const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
   2344:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
   2345:   return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
   2346: }

   2365: function isWednesdayKey(value) {

   2370:   }

   2373: function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {

   2392: function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
   2393:   if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;

   2395:   // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.

   2398:   const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
   2399:     const text=String(value||"");
   2400:     return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
   2401:   });
   2402:   if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);

   2406:   const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];

   2409:   return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
   2410: }
   2411: function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
   2412:   const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
   2413:   const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
   2414:   const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after");
   2415:   const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
   2416:   const seen = new Set();
   2417:   const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
   2418:     const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
   2419:     if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
   2420:   });
   2421:   for (const program of programs) {
   2422:     if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
   2423:     if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
   2424:     const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
   2425:     if (!target) continue;
   2426:     const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
   2427:     const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);

   2828: function inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue='',fileName=''){

   7520: function schoolPeriodStatus(now) {
   7521:   const minute = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
   7522:   const periods = [[520,570],[580,630],[640,690],[700,750],[820,870],[880,930],[940,990]];
   7523:   const index = periods.findIndex(([start, end]) => minute >= start && minute <= end);
   7524:   if (index >= 0) return { index, label: `현재 ${index + 1}교시` };
   7525:   if (minute < periods[0][0]) return { index: -1, label: "일과 시작 전" };
   7526:   if (minute > periods[6][1]) return { index: -1, label: "정규수업 종료" };

    150: function uniqueHeaders(headers) {
    151:   const seen = new Map();
    152:   return headers.map((header, index) => {
    153:     const base = normalizeHeaderText(header) || `__EMPTY_${index + 1}`;
    154:     const count = (seen.get(base) || 0) + 1;
    155:     seen.set(base, count);
    156:     return count === 1 ? base : `${base}__${count}`;
    157:   });
    158: }

    176: function applyCanonicalHeaderAliases(row) {
    177:   if (!row || typeof row !== "object") return row;
    178:   const result = { ...row };
    179:   for (const [canonical, aliases] of Object.entries(CANONICAL_HEADER_ALIASES)) {
    180:     if (String(result[canonical] ?? "").trim()) continue;
    181:     const value = valueByHeader(result, canonical, ...aliases);
    182:     if (value !== "" && value != null) result[canonical] = value;
    183:   }
    184:   return result;
    185: }

    187: function matrixToObjectRows(matrix) {
    188:   const rows = Array.isArray(matrix) ? matrix : [];
    189:   const headerIndex = detectHeaderRowIndex(rows);
    190:   if (headerIndex < 0) return [];
    191: 
    192:   let dataStart = headerIndex + 1;
    193:   let header = (rows[headerIndex] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
    194:   const nextRow = rows[headerIndex + 1] || [];
    195:   const parentHasGroups = header.some(Boolean) && (header.filter(Boolean).length < Math.max(header.length, nextRow.length) * 0.8 || new Set(header.filter(Boolean)).size < header.filter(Boolean).length);
    196:   if (looksLikeSubHeaderRow(nextRow) && parentHasGroups) {
    197:     header = composeTwoLevelHeaders(rows[headerIndex], nextRow);
    198:     dataStart = headerIndex + 2;
    199:   }
    200:   header = uniqueHeaders(header);
    201: 
    202:   return rows.slice(dataStart)
    203:     .filter((row) => Array.isArray(row) && row.some((value) => String(value ?? "").trim()))
    204:     .map((row) => applyCanonicalHeaderAliases(Object.fromEntries(
    205:       header.map((key, index) => [key, row[index] ?? ""]),
    206:     )));
    207: }

    240: function serialToDate(value) {
    241:   const serial = Number(value);
    242:   if (!Number.isFinite(serial) || serial < 20000 || serial > 80000) return "";
    243:   const utc = new Date(Math.round((serial - 25569) * 86400 * 1000));

    245: }

    807:     matchedSlots.forEach(slot=>{
    808:       (program.students||[]).forEach((student,index)=>{
    809:         const status=String(student.result||student.attendance||student.participation||"");
    810:         if(/결석|불참|취소|제외/.test(status)) return;
    811:         const studentId=String(student.studentId||"");
    812:         const studentNo=String(student.studentNo||student.number||"");
    813:         const key=nightRowKey(program.date,slot.label,studentId,studentNo);
    814:         const programRow={
    815:           id:`after-linked-${program.sessionId||program.programId}-${studentId||studentNo}-${slot.label}-${index}`,
    816:           studentId, studentNo, name:String(student.name||""), className:classNumberSafe(student.className||""),
    817:           date:program.date, time:slot.label, type:"방과후학교", affiliation:String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"방과후학교"),
    818:           detail:[program.actualTitle||program.title, program.time, program.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),
    819:           result:"프로그램 인정", originalResult:"", recognizedByProgram:true, programPending:false,
    820:           programTitle:program.actualTitle||program.title, programKind:"after", programId:program.id||"", courseId:program.programId||program.courseId||"", sessionId:program.sessionId||"",
    821:           programType:program.afterType||program.type||"방과후학교", programTime:program.time||"", programPlace:program.place||"", programTeacher:program.teacher||"", programWeekdays:program.weekdays||"",
    822:           inputPath:"12_차시일정·13_출석부 자동연동", submittedAt:"", linkedFromAfterSchool:true,
    823:         };
    824:         const existingIndex=allMappedNightRows.findIndex(row=>nightRowKey(row.date,row.time,row.studentId,row.studentNo)===key);
    825:         if(existingIndex>=0) allMappedNightRows.splice(existingIndex,1,programRow);
    826:         else allMappedNightRows.push(programRow);
    827:         existingNightKeys.add(key);
    828:       });
    829:     });
    830:   });
    831: 
    832:   allMappedNightRows.forEach((row) => {
    833:     if (isAfterSchool(row)) return;
    834:     const program = connectedPrograms.find(item => item.date === row.date && programOverlaps(item,row) && (item.students || []).some(student => String(student.studentId || "") === String(row.studentId || "") || String(student.studentNo || "") === String(row.studentNo || "")));
    835:     if (!program) return;
    836:     const participant = (program.students || []).find(student => String(student.studentId || "") === String(row.studentId || "") || String(student.studentNo || "") === String(row.studentNo || "")) || {};
    837:     const status = String(participant.result || "대상");
    838:     const confirmed = /출석|지각|공결|정상|참여|입실/.test(status) || (!participant.attendance && /참여|등록/.test(String(participant.participation||"")));
    839:     row.originalResult = row.result;
    840:     row.result = confirmed ? "프로그램 인정" : "프로그램 확인";
    841:     row.recognizedByProgram = confirmed;
    842:     row.programPending = !confirmed;
    843:     row.programTitle = program.actualTitle || program.title;
    844:     row.programId = program.id || "";
    845:     row.programKind = program.kind;
    846:     row.type = program.kind === "after" ? "방과후학교" : (program.type || "프로그램");
    847:     row.affiliation = row.programTitle;
    848:     row.detail = [row.programTitle, program.time, program.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    849:   });
    850:   // 방과후 기록도 월간 야자표에서는 상태 표시가 필요하므로 제거하지 않습니다.
    851:   // 결석·미대상 기록은 화면 판정 단계에서 '기록 없음'으로 처리됩니다.
    852:   const nightAttendance = allMappedNightRows;
    853:   afterSchoolAttendance = nightAttendance.filter(row => row.recognizedByProgram || isAfterSchool(row));
    854:   const attendance = nightAttendance.slice(-500).reverse();
    855:   const attended = nightRows.filter((row) =>
    856:     String(row["출결"] || row["입실상태"] || "").includes("출석"),
    857:   ).length;
    858:   const outsideQr = nightRows.filter((row) =>
    859:     String(row["출결"] || "").includes("명단외"),
    860:   ).length;
    861:   // v0.69.2: 공결은 순수 출결 데이터로서 기본정보 연결시트의 30_공결기록만 사용합니다.
    862:   const officialRows = rowsFrom("30_공식출결기록")
    863:     .map((row, index) => ({
    864:       id: String(row["출결ID"] || `official-${index}`).trim(),
    865:       studentId: String(row["학생ID"] || "").trim(),
    866:       studentNo: String(row["학번"] || "").replace(/\.0$/, ""),
    867:       name: String(row["성명"] || "").trim(),
    868:       className: String(row["반"] || "").replace(/\.0$/, ""),
    869:       date: localDate(row["일자"]),

===== END =====
